First run of the tests for ticket #18, before the feature exists. Both parts below
are from the same emulator session, on Pixel_10 (emulator-5554), with no code for
the ticket written yet.

Expected result: every test fails because the feature is missing. The photo screen
shows no text, so it isn't recognised ("On screen: []"), and the app never asks
ML Kit to read the photo. There are no SETUP failures in either part.

Two earlier tries of part 1 are not saved here, because they never reached the
app. All 14 tests stopped in SETUP on an emulator problem: first a stuck Android
notification shade, then Android's "Process system isn't responding" pop-up after
a reboot (backlog item 55). A cold boot, then waiting for the load to drop, cleared
it.

Part 1 of 2: this ticket's new tests
=====================================
